Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in video theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 928 and the invariant I(A,B) = 303 mod 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the video space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 928 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 303. QED.
